(1) two means of knowing the user: a fix, joined with the ruling on the spec verdict's first risk: each program reads the user it runs as once at its composition root and hands that value to the directory check, every state-file read and every state-file write; the means depends on no writable directory (a safe call of a crate the lock already holds, requirement 4), so an unusable temporary directory fails no start; closest alternative: keep the probe file and name the temporary directory in its error. (2) the rule held once a program: stands, the requirement allowed it; closest alternative: one function in estafette-trace, which widens that package beyond paths. (3) check-then-use windows: stand as accepted, they open only where the user points TMPDIR at a world-writable directory without the sticky bit; into the change entry; closest alternative: the state files move into the private runtime directory, which makes the bridge create that directory. (4) one command_failed event per poll while a directory is refused: stands, ruled 2026-11-20. (5) a staging file left by a killed bridge fails one write: stands, it is the refusal of anything planted; into the change entry; closest alternative: the write takes the next staging name within the same call.
